    ## Naming Convention (Restored)
    Format: {STATUS}-{DATE}-{THUMBPRINT}-{AxTnn}-{TYPE}-{Title}.md
    - A = Priority letter (A/B/C/D/Z/--)

    - T = Type letter (E/F/S/T/B)
    - nn = Story points (01-99, zero-padded)

    Examples:
    - NEW-251119-InfoSec-Magic-A1E34-EPIC.md (Priority A1, Type Epic, 34 pts)
    - NEW-251119-InfoSec-Magic-A1T02-TASK-CredMagic-Tests.md (Priority A1, Type Task, 2 pts)

    - Migrated 100+ tickets from BUCKETS/ to flat thumbprint naming
    - Preserved git history with git mv for 49 BACKLOG tickets
    - Moved all files from nested EPIC directories to status folders
    - Removed BUCKETS/ directory structure (Windows MAX_PATH fix)

    ## Structure
    - BACKLOG/: New work items (NEW-* prefix)
    - IN-PROGRESS/: Active work (OPEN-*, READY-*, WIP-* prefix)
    - CLOSED/: Completed work (CLOSED-*, DONE-* prefix)

    ## Thumbprint Families (git grep enabled)
    - 251110-Dashboard-Git: 8 tickets (EPIC + 7 FEATs/ENHANCEs)
    - 251111-Onboarding: 10 tickets (EPIC + 9 STORYs/ENHANCEs)
    - 251115-Dashboard-Features: 8 tickets (4 FEATs + 4 STORYs)
    - 251115-Deploy-Data: 7 tickets (FEAT + STORY + 5 TASKs)
